See
H.3

Proof of
Lemma

H.3
.

Firstly note that, from
Lemma

H.1
, the left-hand-side of
Eq. (
74
)
is equivalent to

\addcontentsline{lla}{section}{\numberline q:fourtet2}\lvert\En^{M_{\texttt{lat}},\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}[f(s_{h},a_{h})]-\widetilde{\En}^{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}_{\phi}[f(s_{h},a_{h})]\rvert=\lvert\En^{M_{\texttt{lat}},\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}[f(s_{h},a_{h})]-\En^{M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}},\pi_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi}\left[[f\circ\phi](x_{h},a_{h})\right]\rvert

(78)

For any
{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}:\mathcal{S}\times[H]\rightarrow\Delta(\mathcal{A})
, let
d_{\texttt{lat},h}^{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}=d^{M_{\texttt{lat}},{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}}_{h}
denote the occupancy in
M_{\texttt{lat}}
, and similarly for any
\pi_{\texttt{obs}}:\mathcal{X}\times[H]\rightarrow\Delta(\mathcal{A})
let
d_{\texttt{obs},h}^{\pi_{\texttt{obs}}}(x_{h},a_{h})=d^{M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}},{\pi_{\texttt{obs}}}}_{h}(x_{h},a_{h})
denote the occupancy in
M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}}
. We overload notation by letting
d_{\texttt{obs},h}^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi}(s,a)\coloneqq\sum_{x:\phi(x)=s}d_{\texttt{obs},h}^{\pi\circ\phi}(x,a)
. We will establish the stronger result that

\addcontentsline{lla}{section}{\numberline q:direct}\left\|d_{\texttt{lat},h}^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}}(\cdot)-d_{\texttt{obs},h}^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi}(\cdot)\right\|_{\texttt{tv}}\leq\sum_{h^{\prime}<h}\En^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi}\left[\left\|\left[P_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi\right](x_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-\phi\sharp P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}}(x_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})\right\|_{\texttt{tv}}\right],

(79)

where the
tv
norm on the left-hand-side is over
\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
. Note that this implies the desired bound on
Eq. (
78
)
by Holder’s inequality. We prove this by induction over
h
. For the base case (
h=0
), we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{1},a_{1}}\Bigg|d^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}}_{\texttt{lat},1}(s_{1},a_{1})-d^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi}_{\texttt{obs}}(s_{1},a_{1})\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{s_{1},a_{1}}\Bigg|P_{\texttt{lat},0}(s_{1}\mid\emptyset){\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}(a_{1}\mid s_{1})-\sum_{x_{1}=\phi(x_{1})=s_{1}}d^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi}_{\texttt{obs}}(x_{1},a_{1})\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{s_{1},a_{1}}\Bigg|P_{\texttt{lat},0}(s_{1}\mid\emptyset){\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}(a_{1}\mid s_{1})-\sum_{x_{1}=\phi(x_{1})=s_{1}}P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},0}(x_{1}\mid\emptyset){\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}(a_{1}\mid\phi(x_{1}))\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{s_{1}}\Bigg|P_{\texttt{lat},0}(s_{1}\mid\emptyset)-\phi_{1}\sharp P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},0}(s_{1}\mid\emptyset)\Bigg|\sum_{a_{1}}{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}(a_{1}\mid s_{1})

\displaystyle\qquad=\left\|P_{\texttt{lat},0}(\emptyset)-\phi_{1}\sharp P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},0}(\emptyset)\right\|_{\texttt{tv}}.

For the general case, let us further overload notation by letting
d^{\pi\circ\phi}_{\texttt{obs},h}(s_{h})=\sum_{a_{h}}d^{\pi\circ\phi}_{\texttt{obs},h}(s_{h},a_{h})
and
P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}}(s_{h}\mid x_{h-1},a_{h-1})=\phi\sharp P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}}(s_{h}\mid x_{h-1},a_{h-1})=\sum_{x_{h}:\phi(x_{h})=s_{h}}P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}}(x_{h}\mid x_{h-1},a_{h-1})
. Let us also abbreviate
\pi\coloneqq{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}
. Firstly note that it is sufficient to establish the
result for
\sum_{s_{h}\in\mathcal{S}}\left\lvert d_{\texttt{lat},h}^{\pi}(s_{h})-d^{\pi\circ\phi}_{\texttt{obs},h}(s_{h})\right\rvert
, since

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{h},a_{h}\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}\left\lvert d_{\texttt{lat},h}^{\pi}(s_{h},a_{h})-d^{\pi\circ\phi}_{\texttt{obs},h}(s_{h},a_{h})\right\rvert

\displaystyle=\sum_{s_{h},a_{h}\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}\left\lvert d_{\texttt{lat},h}^{\pi}(s_{h})-d^{\pi\circ\phi}_{\texttt{obs},h}(s_{h})\right\rvert\pi(a_{h}\mid s_{h})

\displaystyle=\sum_{s_{h}\in\mathcal{S}}\left\lvert d_{\texttt{lat},h}^{\pi}(s_{h})-d^{\pi\circ\phi}_{\texttt{obs},h}(s_{h})\right\rvert.

Below, all summations over
s_{h}
(resp.
x_{h}
) with domain unspecified
are over
\mathcal{S}
(resp.
\mathcal{X}
), and likewise for summations over

s_{h},a_{h}
or
x_{h},a_{h}
. We have:

\displaystyle\sum_{s_{h}}\Bigg|d^{\pi}_{\texttt{lat},h}(s_{h})-d_{\texttt{obs},h}^{\pi\circ\phi}(s_{h})\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\sum_{s_{h}}\Bigg|d_{\texttt{lat},h}^{\pi}(s_{h})-\sum_{x_{h}:\phi(x_{h})=s_{h}}d_{\texttt{obs},h}^{\pi\circ\phi}(x_{h})\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\sum_{s_{h}}\Bigg|\sum_{s_{h-1},a_{h-1}}d_{\texttt{lat},h}^{\pi}(s_{h-1},a_{h-1})P_{\texttt{lat},h}(s_{h}\mid s_{h-1},a_{h-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt-\sum_{x_{h}:\phi(x_{h})=s_{h}}\sum_{x_{h-1},a_{h-1}}d_{\texttt{obs},h}^{\pi\circ\phi}(x_{h-1},a_{h-1})P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h}(x_{h}\mid x_{h-1},a_{h-1})\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\sum_{s_{h}}\Bigg|\sum_{s_{h-1},a_{h-1}}d_{\texttt{lat},h}^{\pi}(s_{h-1},a_{h-1})P_{\texttt{lat},h}(s_{h}\mid s_{h-1},a_{h-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 100.00015pt-\sum_{x_{h-1},a_{h-1}}d_{\texttt{obs},h}^{\pi\circ\phi}(x_{h-1},a_{h-1})P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h}(s_{h}\mid x_{h-1},a_{h-1})\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\sum_{s_{h}}\Bigg|\sum_{s_{h-1},a_{h-1}}d_{\texttt{lat},h}^{\pi}(s_{h-1},a_{h-1})P_{\texttt{lat},h}(s_{h}\mid s_{h-1},a_{h-1})-\sum_{x_{h-1},a_{h-1}}d_{\texttt{obs},h}^{\pi\circ\phi}(x_{h-1},a_{h-1})P_{\texttt{lat},h}(s_{h}\mid\phi(x_{h-1}),a_{h-1})

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{x_{h-1},a_{h-1}}d_{\texttt{obs},h}^{\pi\circ\phi}(x_{h-1},a_{h-1})P_{\texttt{lat},h}(s_{h}\mid\phi(x_{h-1}),a_{h-1})-\sum_{x_{h-1},a_{h-1}}d_{\texttt{obs},h}^{\pi\circ\phi}(x_{h-1},a_{h-1})P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h}(s_{h}\mid x_{h-1},a_{h-1})\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s_{h-1},a_{h-1}}\left\lvert d_{\texttt{lat},h}^{\pi}(s_{h-1},a_{h-1})-\sum_{x_{h-1}:\phi(x_{h-1})=s_{h-1}}d_{\texttt{obs},h}^{\pi\circ\phi}(x_{h-1},a_{h-1})\right\rvert\sum_{s_{h}}P_{\texttt{lat},h}(s_{h}\mid s_{h-1},a_{h-1})

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{s_{h}}\left\lvert\sum_{x_{h-1},a_{h-1}}d_{\texttt{obs},h}^{\pi\circ\phi}(x_{h-1},a_{h-1})\left((P_{\texttt{lat},h}\circ\phi)(s_{h}\mid x_{h-1},a_{h-1})-P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h}(s_{h}\mid x_{h-1},a_{h-1})\right)\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\left\|d_{\texttt{lat},h-1}^{\pi}(\cdot)-d_{\texttt{obs},h-1}^{\pi\circ\phi}(\phi^{-1}(\cdot))\right\|_{\texttt{tv}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{x_{h-1},a_{h-1}}d_{\texttt{obs},h}^{\pi\circ\phi}(x_{h-1},a_{h-1})\sum_{s_{h}}\Bigg|(P_{\texttt{lat},h}\circ\phi)(s_{h}\mid x_{h-1},a_{h-1})-P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h}(s_{h}\mid x_{h-1},a_{h-1})\Bigg|

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\|d_{\texttt{lat},h-1}^{\pi}(\cdot)-d_{\texttt{obs},h-1}^{\pi\circ\phi}(\phi^{-1}(\cdot))\right\|_{\texttt{tv}}+\En^{\pi\circ\phi}\left[\left\|\left[P_{\texttt{lat},h}\circ\phi\right](x_{h-1},a_{h-1})-\phi\sharp P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h}(x_{h-1},a_{h-1})\right\|_{\texttt{tv}}\right].

From which it follows that, for each
h
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\|d_{\texttt{lat},h}^{\pi}(\cdot)-d_{\texttt{obs},h}^{\pi\circ\phi}(\phi^{-1}(\cdot))\right\|_{\texttt{tv}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{h^{\prime}<h}\En^{\pi\circ\phi}\left[\left\|\left[P_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi\right]_{h^{\prime}}(x_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-\phi_{h^{\prime}+1}\sharp P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h^{\prime}}(x_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})\right\|_{\texttt{tv}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{h^{\prime}\in[H]}\En^{\pi\circ\phi}\left[\left\|\left[P_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi\right]_{h^{\prime}}(x_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-\phi_{h^{\prime}+1}\sharp P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h^{\prime}}(x_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})\right\|_{\texttt{tv}}\right].

∎

H.3
Proofs for
Section

H.1.3
: Risk Bound Under
CorruptionRobustness
(
Theorem

G.1
)

See
G.1

Proof of
Theorem

G.1
.

Let us write
\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,K+1)}}_{\texttt{lat}}=\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}
and, for any
t,k\in[T]\times[K+1]
,
\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}_{\texttt{obs}}\coloneqq\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}
. Let
p^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}_{\texttt{obs}}
denote the distributions of played policies
\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}_{\texttt{obs}}
induced by the interaction of
Alg
{}_{\texttt{lat}}
and
Rep
{}_{\texttt{self};\texttt{opt}}
inside the
O2L
algorithm. Let us write the online sum of self-prediction errors as

\addcontentsline{lla}{section}{\numberline q:epsrep}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}^{2}\coloneqq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{K+1}\sum_{h=0}^{H}\En_{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}_{\texttt{obs}}\sim p^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}}\En^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}_{\texttt{obs}}}\left[D^{2}_{\mathsf{H}}\left(\left[M^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\scriptscriptstyle{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}\right]_{h}(x_{h},a_{h}),\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\scriptscriptstyle{h+1}}\sharp M^{\star}_{\scriptscriptstyle{\texttt{obs},h}}(x_{h},a_{h})\right)\right]

(80)

Since the final output policy of
O2L
satisfies
\widehat{\pi}_{\texttt{lat}}=\texttt{Unif}(\widehat{\pi}_{\texttt{lat}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(1)}},\ldots,\widehat{\pi}_{\texttt{lat}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(T)}})
(
12
), we have

\En\left[\texttt{Risk}_{\texttt{obs}}(TK)\right]=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\En\left[J^{M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}}}({\pi^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}}})-J^{M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}}}(\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{obs}})\right].

We take the following decomposition on the risk

\displaystyle J^{M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}}}(\pi^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}})-J^{M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}}}(\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{obs}})

\displaystyle=J^{M^{\star}_{\texttt{lat}}}(\pi_{M^{\star}_{\texttt{lat}}})-J^{M^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}}(\pi_{M^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}})+\underbrace{J^{M^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}}(\pi_{M^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}})-J^{M^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}}(\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}})}_{\mathrm{A_{t}}}+\underbrace{J^{M^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}}(\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}})-J^{M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}}}(\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{obs}})}_{\mathrm{B_{t}}}.

(81)

We will show that
\En\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{A_{t}}\right]\lesssim T\texttt{Reg}_{\texttt{base}}(K)+\alpha\sqrt{T}\En[\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}]
and that
\En\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathrm{B_{t}}\right]\lesssim\sqrt{TH}\En[\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}]
, then return to the first
term
J^{M^{\star}_{\texttt{lat}}}(\pi_{M^{\star}_{\texttt{lat}}})-J^{M^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}}(\pi_{M^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}})
at the end of the proof.

To bound
\En\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}A_{t}\right]
, we note that

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\En\left[A_{t}\right]

\displaystyle\leq c_{1}T\texttt{Risk}_{\texttt{base}}(K)+\alpha\sum_{t=1}^{T}\En\left[\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\En_{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}_{\texttt{lat}}\sim p^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}_{\texttt{lat}}}\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}_{\texttt{lat}}}_{\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}\left[D^{2}_{\mathsf{H}}\left(M_{\texttt{lat},h}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}(s_{h},a_{h}),\widetilde{M}^{\star}_{\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}},h}(s_{1:h},a_{1:h})\right)\right]}\right]

\displaystyle\leq c_{1}T\texttt{Risk}_{\texttt{base}}(K)+\alpha\sum_{t=1}^{T}\En\left[\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\En_{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}_{\texttt{lat}}\sim p^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}_{\texttt{lat}}}\En^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}\left[[\Delta_{h}(M^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}},\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}})](x_{h},a_{h})\right]}\right]

\displaystyle\leq c_{1}T\texttt{Risk}_{\texttt{base}}(K)+\alpha\sqrt{T}\En\left[\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\En_{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}_{\texttt{obs}}\sim p^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}_{\texttt{obs}}}\En^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}_{\texttt{obs}}}\left[[\Delta_{h}(M^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}},\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}})](x_{h},a_{h})\right]}\right]

\displaystyle\leq c_{1}T\texttt{Risk}_{\texttt{base}}(K)+\alpha\sqrt{T}\En\left[\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}\right].

where the first line follows from the
CorruptionRobust
definition (
Definition

H.2
), the second line follows from
Lemma

H.2
, the third line follows by Cauchy-Schwartz, and the last line recalls the definition of
\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}
from
Eq. (
80
)
.

For the term
\sum_{t=1}^{T}B_{t}
, for any
{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}:\mathcal{S}\times[H]\rightarrow\Delta(\mathcal{A})
we let
Q_{\texttt{lat}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}},h}^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}}=\mathcal{T}^{M_{\texttt{lat}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{h}Q_{\texttt{lat}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}},h+1}^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}}
be the
Q^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}}
function of the latent MDP
M_{\texttt{lat}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}
. Note that

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{J^{M^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}}(\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}})-\En^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}\left[[Q_{\scriptscriptstyle{\texttt{lat}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}}^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}]_{1}(x_{1},a_{1})\right]\right\}

(82)

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\En^{M^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}},\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}}\left[Q_{\scriptscriptstyle{\texttt{lat}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}},1}}^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}(s_{1},a_{1})\right]-\En^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}\left[[Q_{\scriptscriptstyle{\texttt{lat}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}}^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}]_{1}(x_{1},a_{1})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\En^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}\left[\left\|[P^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\scriptscriptstyle{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}]_{0}(\emptyset)-\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\scriptscriptstyle{1}}\sharp P^{\star}_{\scriptscriptstyle{\texttt{obs},0}}(\emptyset)\right\|_{\texttt{tv}}\right]

(by
Lemma

H.3
)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=0}^{H}\En^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}\left[\left\|[P^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\scriptscriptstyle{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}]_{h}(x_{h},a_{h})-\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\scriptscriptstyle{h+1}}\sharp P^{\star}_{\scriptscriptstyle{\texttt{obs},h}}(x_{h},a_{h})\right\|_{\texttt{tv}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sqrt{TH}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}},

(by Cauchy-Schwartz)

so it is enough to bound

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\En^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}\left[[Q_{\scriptscriptstyle{\texttt{lat}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}}^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}]_{1}(x_{1},a_{1})\right]-J^{M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}}}(\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{obs}})\right\}.

Fix
t
and
h
, whose indexing we omit below for cleanliness. Note that, for any
{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}:\mathcal{S}\times[H]\rightarrow\Delta(\mathcal{A})
, we have:

\displaystyle\En^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi}\left[\left(\left[Q_{\texttt{lat}}^{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi\right]_{h}(x_{h},a_{h})-\mathcal{T}^{M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}},{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi}_{h}\left[Q_{\texttt{lat}}^{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi\right]_{h+1}(x_{h},a_{h})\right)^{2}\right]

(83)

\displaystyle\,\leq 2\En^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi}\left[\left(\left[r_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi\right]_{h}-r^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h}\right)^{2}(x_{h},a_{h})\right]

(84)

\displaystyle\quad+2\En^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi}\left[\left(\En_{P_{\texttt{lat},h}(\phi(x_{h}),a_{h})}\left[Q^{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}_{\texttt{lat},h+1}(\cdot,{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}})\right]-\En_{P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h}(x_{h},a_{h})}\left[\left[Q_{\texttt{lat}}^{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi\right]_{h+1}(\cdot,{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}})\right]\right)^{2}\right]

(85)

\displaystyle\,\leq 2\En^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi}\left[\left(\left[r_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi\right]_{h}-r^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h}\right)^{2}(x_{h},a_{h})+\left\|P_{\texttt{lat},h}(\phi(x_{h}),a_{h})-\phi_{h+1}\sharp P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h}(x_{h},a_{h})\right\|^{2}_{\texttt{tv}}\right]

(86)

\displaystyle\,\leq 4\En^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi}\left[D^{2}_{\mathsf{H}}\left(M_{\texttt{lat},h}(\phi_{h}(x_{h}),a_{h}),\phi_{h+1}\sharp M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h}(x_{h},a_{h})\right)\right],

(87)

where the final line follows from two applications of the
data-processing inequality (since
M_{\texttt{lat},h}(r_{h},s_{h+1}\mid\phi_{h}(x_{h}),a_{h})=R_{\texttt{lat},h}(r_{h}\mid\phi_{h}(x_{h}),a_{h})P_{\texttt{lat},h}(s_{h+1}\mid\phi_{h}(x_{h}),a_{h})
and
\phi_{h+1}\sharp M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h}(r_{h},s_{h+1}\mid x_{h},a_{h})=R^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h}(r_{h}\mid x_{h},a_{h})\phi_{h+1}\sharp P^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h}(s_{h+1}\mid x_{h},a_{h})
) as well as the bound
\left\|p-q\right\|^{2}_{\texttt{tv}}\leq D^{2}_{\mathsf{H}}\left(p,q\right)
.
Summing this over
t,h
and using a standard decomposition for regret
(
Lemma

B.6
) gives:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\En^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}\left[[Q_{\scriptscriptstyle{\texttt{lat}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}}^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}]_{1}(x_{1},a_{1})\right]-J^{M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}}}(\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{obs}})\right\}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\En^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}\left[[Q_{\scriptscriptstyle{\texttt{lat}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}}^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}]_{h}(x_{h},a_{h})-\mathcal{T}^{M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}},\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{h}[Q_{\scriptscriptstyle{\texttt{lat}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}}^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}]_{h+1}(x_{h},a_{h})\right]

(
Lemma

B.6
)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sqrt{TH}\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\En^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}\left[\left([Q_{\scriptscriptstyle{\texttt{lat}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}}^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}]_{h}(x_{h},a_{h})-\mathcal{T}^{M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}},\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{h}[Q_{\scriptscriptstyle{\texttt{lat}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}}^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}]_{h+1}(x_{h},a_{h})\right)^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sqrt{4TH}\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\En^{\widehat{\pi}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}\left[D^{2}_{\mathsf{H}}\left(\left[M^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat},h}\circ\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\scriptscriptstyle{h}}\right](x_{h},a_{h}),\phi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\scriptscriptstyle{h+1}}\sharp M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs},h}(x_{h},a_{h})\right)\right]}

(By
Eq. (
87
)
)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sqrt{4TH}\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}.

Returning to the decomposition of
Eq. (
81
)
and combining everything gives:

\displaystyle\En\left[\texttt{Risk}_{\texttt{obs}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\left\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\En\left[J^{M^{\star}_{\texttt{lat}}}(\pi_{M^{\star}_{\texttt{lat}}})-J^{M_{\texttt{lat}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}(\pi_{M^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{lat}}})\right]\right\}+\frac{1}{T}\left(\alpha\sqrt{T}+4\sqrt{TH}\right)\En[\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}]+c_{1}\cdot\texttt{Risk}_{\texttt{base}}(K)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\left\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\En\left[J(\pi^{\star})-J^{M_{\texttt{lat}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}(\pi_{M_{\texttt{lat}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}})+\gamma\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}^{2}\right]\right\}+\frac{\gamma^{-1}}{T}\left(\alpha\sqrt{T}+4\sqrt{TH}\right)^{2}+c_{1}\cdot\texttt{Risk}_{\texttt{base}}(K)

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\frac{2K}{T}\texttt{Est}_{\texttt{self};\texttt{opt}}(T,\gamma)+2\gamma^{-1}\left(\alpha^{2}+16H\right)+c_{1}\cdot\texttt{Risk}_{\texttt{base}}(K),

where the second inequality follows by AM-GM applied to the middle term and the third inequality follows from: i) Jensen’s inequality, ii)
Assumption

4.3
applied to the distributions
\bar{p}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{\texttt{obs}}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}p^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t,k)}}_{\texttt{obs}}
, iii) the bound
K+1\leq 2K
, and iv) the inequality
(x+y)^{2}\leq 2(x^{2}+y^{2})
.
∎

H.4
Proofs for
Section

H.1.4
: Examples of
CorruptionRobust
Algorithms

See
H.1

Proof of
Theorem

H.1
.
Recall that the agent is observing data from the
\phi
-compressed POMDP
\widetilde{M}^{\star}_{\phi}
, and thus the datasets are of the form
\mathcal{D}_{h}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}=\mathcal{D}_{\phi,h}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}=\{\phi(x^{\scriptscriptstyle{(i)}}_{h}),a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(i)}}_{h},r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(i)}}_{h},\phi(x^{\scriptscriptstyle{(i)}}_{h+1})\}_{i=1}^{k-1}
. For any
{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}\in\Pi_{\texttt{lat}}
, we define

\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}}_{\phi,h}f(s_{h},a_{h})=\tilde{r}^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}}_{\phi,h}(s_{h},a_{h})+\En_{s^{\prime}\sim\widetilde{P}^{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}_{\phi,h}(s_{h},a_{h})}\left[f(s^{\prime})\right],

where
\tilde{r}^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}}_{\phi,h}
and
\widetilde{P}^{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}_{\phi,h}
are the policy-dependent Markov operators defined in
Eq. (
66
)
and
Eq. (
68
)
.

As a consequence, we observe the following misspecification guarantee for
\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat}}
.

Lemma H.4

(Misspecification guarantee for

\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat}}
)
.

\forall f:\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\rightarrow[0,1]:\quad\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}\left[\left(\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat},h}f(s_{h},a_{h})-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi,h}f(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}^{2}).

Proof of
Lemma

H.4
.

Follows from
Assumption

H.2
and the definitions of
\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi,h}
and
\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat},h}
.
∎

We recall that
Assumption

H.2
implies the following simulation lemma from
M_{\texttt{lat}}
to
\widetilde{M}^{\star}_{\phi}
.

Lemma H.5

(Simulation lemma)
.

\forall f:\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\rightarrow[0,1]\quad\left\lvert\En^{M_{\texttt{lat}},{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}}\left[f(s_{h},a_{h})\right]-\widetilde{\En}^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}}_{\phi}\left[f(s_{h},a_{h})\right]\right\rvert\leq\sum_{h^{\prime}<h}\widetilde{\En}^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}}_{\phi}\left[\left\|P_{\texttt{lat},h^{\prime}}(s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-\widetilde{P}^{{\pi_{\texttt{lat}}}}_{\phi,h^{\prime}}(s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})\right\|_{\texttt{tv}}\right]

and thus

\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{K}\En^{M_{\texttt{lat}},\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}\left[f(s_{h},a_{h})\right]\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{K}\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}\left[f(s_{h},a_{h})\right]+H^{3/2}\sqrt{K\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}^{2}}.

We begin with the following lemmas, which will be proved in the sequel.

Lemma H.6

(Optimism)
.

For the choice of
\beta
in
Theorem

H.1
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have that for all
k\in[K]
:

Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat}}\in\mathcal{F}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}.

Lemma H.7

(Small in-sample squared Bellman errors)
.

With probability at least
1-\delta
, we have that for all
k\in[K]
,
h\in[H]
, and
f\in\mathcal{F}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}
:

\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(i)}}}_{\phi}\left[\left(f(s_{h},a_{h})-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(i)}}}_{\phi,h}f(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\mathcal{O}(\beta).

Let us write
\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{\texttt{obs}}\coloneqq\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}\circ\phi
. Let us introduce the shorthand
\tilde{d}_{\texttt{obs},h}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}\coloneqq\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}d^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{\texttt{obs}}}_{\texttt{obs},h}
, where
d^{\pi}_{\texttt{obs}}
is the occupancy for
M^{\star}_{\texttt{obs}}
, and also the burn-in time

\kappa_{h}(x,a)\coloneqq\min\left\{k:\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}d^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\texttt{obs},h}(x,a)\geq C_{\texttt{cov}}\mu^{\star}_{h}(x,a)\right\}.

Let us recall, from the analysis of
[
XFBJK23
]
, that for any
h\in[H]
and
f:\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\rightarrow[0,1]
we have

\addcontentsline{lla}{section}{\numberline q:burn-in-time}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\En^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}\left[f(s_{h},a_{h})\mathbb{I}\left\{k<\kappa_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})\right\}\right]\leq 2C_{\texttt{cov}},

(88)

as well as

\addcontentsline{lla}{section}{\numberline q:cov-potential}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{s,a}\frac{(d^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{\texttt{obs}}}_{h}(x,a)\mathbb{I}\{k\geq\kappa_{h}(x,a)\})^{2}}{\tilde{d}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h}(x,a)}\leq O\left(HC_{\texttt{cov}}\log(K)\right).

(89)

\displaystyle\sum_{k}J^{M_{\texttt{lat}}}(\pi_{M_{\texttt{lat}}})-J^{M_{\texttt{lat}}}(\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}})

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\En^{M_{\texttt{lat}},\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}\left[f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}(s_{h},a_{h})-\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat}}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}(s_{h},a_{h})\right]

(Optimism (
Lemma

H.6
))

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}\left[f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}(s_{h},a_{h})-\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat}}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}(s_{h},a_{h})\right]+H^{3/2}\sqrt{K\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}^{2}}

(
Lemma

H.5
)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\En^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}\circ\phi}\left[\left[(f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}-\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat}}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}})\circ\phi\right](x_{h},a_{h})\right]+H^{3/2}\sqrt{K\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}^{2}}

(
Lemma

H.1
)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\En^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}\circ\phi}\left[\left[(f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}-\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat}}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}})\circ\phi\right](x_{h},a_{h})\mathbb{I}\{k\geq\kappa_{h}(x_{h},a_{h})\}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+2HC_{\texttt{cov}}+H^{3/2}\sqrt{K\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}^{2}}

(burn-in time
Eq. (
88
)
)

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\En^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}\circ\phi}\left[\left[(f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi,h}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}})\circ\phi\right](x_{h},a_{h})\mathbb{I}\{k\geq\kappa_{h}(x_{h},a_{h})\}\right]}_{\mathrm{(I)}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\En^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}\circ\phi}\left[\left[(\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi,h}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}-\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat},h}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}})\circ\phi\right](x_{h},a_{h})\right]}_{\mathrm{(II)}}+2HC_{\texttt{cov}}+H^{3/2}\sqrt{K\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}^{2}}

Note that, by change of measure (
Lemma

H.1
) and the misspecification guarantee (
Lemma

H.4
), the second term is bounded by:

\mathrm{(II)}=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}\left[(\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi,h}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}-\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat},h}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}})(s_{h},a_{h})\right]\leq\sqrt{KH\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}^{2}}.

Turning to the first term, we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\En^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{\texttt{obs}}}\left[\left[(f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi,h}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}})\circ\phi\right](x_{h},a_{h})\mathbb{I}\{k\geq\kappa_{h}(x_{h},a_{h})\}\right]

(90)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\leq\sqrt{\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{x,a}\frac{(d^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{\texttt{obs}}}_{h}(x,a)\mathbb{I}\{k\geq\kappa_{h}(x,a)\})^{2}}{\tilde{d}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h}(x,a)}}\sqrt{\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\En_{\tilde{d}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{\texttt{obs}}}\left[\left((f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi,h}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}})\circ\phi\right)^{2}(x_{h},a_{h})\right]}

(91)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\leq\sqrt{HC_{\texttt{cov}}\log(K)}\sqrt{\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\En_{\tilde{d}^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{\texttt{obs}}}\left[\left((f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi,h}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}})\circ\phi\right)^{2}(x_{h},a_{h})\right]}

(coverability potential
Eq. (
89
)
)

\displaystyle\quad\quad=\sqrt{HC_{\texttt{cov}}\log(K)}\sqrt{\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(i)}}}_{\phi}\left[\left(f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}(s_{h},a_{h})-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi,h}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{2}\right]}

(change of measure,
Lemma

H.1
)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\leq\mathcal{O}\left(H\sqrt{C_{\texttt{cov}}K\log(K)\beta}\right),

(92)

where we have used that, from
Lemma

H.7
, we have:

\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(i)}}}_{\phi}\left[\left(f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}(s_{h},a_{h})-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(i)}}}_{\phi}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\mathcal{O}(\beta HK).

This gives an upper bound on the regret of

\sum_{k=1}^{K}J^{M_{\texttt{lat}}}(\pi_{M_{\texttt{lat}}})-J^{M_{\texttt{lat}}}(\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}})\leq\mathcal{O}\left(H\sqrt{C_{\texttt{cov}}K\log(K)\beta}+H^{3/2}\sqrt{K\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}^{2}}\right).

Using that
\beta=\mathcal{O}\left(\log(\frac{\abs{\cF}\abs{\cG}HK}{\delta})+\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}\right)
and simplifying gives

\sum_{k=1}^{K}J^{M_{\texttt{lat}}}(\pi_{M_{\texttt{lat}}})-J^{M_{\texttt{lat}}}(\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}})\leq\mathcal{O}\left(H\sqrt{C_{\texttt{cov}}K\log(K)\log(\nicefrac{\abs{\cF}\abs{\cG}HK}{\delta})}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(H^{3/2}\sqrt{KC_{\texttt{cov}}\log(K)\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}^{2}}\right),

as desired. It only remains to establish the concentrations results.

Concentration analysis.

We establish the concentration results of
Lemma

H.6
and
Lemma

H.7
.

Proof of
Lemma

H.7
.

Let

X_{k}(h,f)=\left(f_{h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h}-f_{h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}-\left(\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}f_{h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h}-f_{h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}.

Let
\mathfrak{F}_{k,h}=\{s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(i)}}_{1},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(i)}}_{1},r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(i)}}_{1},\ldots,s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(i)}}_{H},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(i)}}_{H},r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(i)}}_{H}\}_{i=1}^{k}
. Note that

\displaystyle\En\left[r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h}+f_{h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h+1})\mid\mathfrak{F}_{k,h}\right]

\displaystyle=\En\left[r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h}+f_{h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h+1})\mid\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}\right]

\displaystyle=\En\left[\En\left[r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h}+f_{h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h+1})\mid s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h},\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}\right]\mid\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}\right]

\displaystyle=\En\left[\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}f(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h})\mid\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}\right]

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}\left[\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}f(s_{h},a_{h})\right],

and thus that

\En\left[X_{k}(h,f)\mid\mathfrak{F}_{k,h}\right]=\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}\left[\left(f_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}f_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{2}\right].

Next, note that

\displaystyle\texttt{Var}\left[X_{k}(h,f)\mid\mathfrak{F}_{k,h}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\En\left[\left(X_{k}(h,f)\right)^{2}\mid\mathfrak{F}_{k,h}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 16\En\left[\left(f_{h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h})-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}f_{h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h})\right)^{2}\mid\mathfrak{F}_{k,h}\right]

\displaystyle=16\En\left[X_{k}(h,f)\mid\mathfrak{F}_{k,h}\right].

By Freedman’s inequality (
Lemma

B.2
,
Lemma

B.3
), we have that with probability at least
1-\delta
:

\left\lvert\sum_{t<k}X_{t}(h,f)-\sum_{t<k}\En\left[X_{t}(h,f)\mid\mathfrak{F}_{t,h}\right]\right\rvert\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)\sum_{t<k}\En\left[X_{t}(h,f)\mid\mathfrak{F}_{t,h}\right]}+\log(1/\delta)\right)

Taking a union bound over
[K]\times[H]\times\mathcal{F}
, we have that for all
k,h,f
, with probability at least
1-\delta
:

\displaystyle\left\lvert\sum_{t<k}X_{t}(h,f)-\sum_{t<k}\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}\left[\left(f_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}f_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{2}\right]\right\rvert

(93)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\iota\sum_{t<k}\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}\left[\left(f_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}f_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{2}\right]}+\iota\right),

(94)

where
\iota=\log(\abs*{\cF}HK/\delta)
. We now show that

\addcontentsline{lla}{section}{\numberline q:latent-golf-x-t-bounded}\sum_{t<k}X_{t}(h,f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}})\leq\beta+\mathcal{O}\left(\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}+\iota\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\beta\right),

(95)

which will imply, from
Eq. (
93
)
, that

\sum_{t<k}\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}\left[\left(f_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}f_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\iota+\beta\right)=\mathcal{O}(\beta),

as desired. To see
Eq. (
95
)
, let

\Delta_{k}=\sum_{t<k}\left(\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat}}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h}-f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}-\left(\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{\phi}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h}-f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}

and then note that:

\displaystyle\sum_{t<k}X_{t}(h,f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}})

\displaystyle=\sum_{t<k}\left(f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h}-f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}-\left(\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{\phi}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h}-f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t<k}\left(f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h}-f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}-\left(\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat}}f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h}-f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}+\Delta_{k}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t<k}\left(f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h}-f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}-\inf_{g_{h}\in\mathcal{G}_{h}}\sum_{t<k}\left(g(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h}-f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}+\Delta_{k}

\displaystyle\leq\beta+\Delta_{k}.

where the second-to-last line follows from
\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat}}\mathcal{F}\subseteq\mathcal{G}
and the last line follows from the definition of the confidence set. It remains to show that
\Delta_{k}\leq\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}+\iota)
, which we do via a similar concentration argument. Namely, let

Y_{t}(h,f)=\left(\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat}}f_{h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h}-f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}-\left(\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{\phi}f_{h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h}-f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}_{h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2},

and note that, as before,

\En\left[Y_{t}(h,f)\mid\mathfrak{F}_{t,h}\right]=\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{\phi}\left[\left(\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat}}f_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{\phi}f_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{2}\right],

and

\texttt{Var}\left[Y_{t}(h,f)\mid\mathfrak{F}_{t,h}\right]\leq 16\En\left[Y_{t}(h,f)\mid\mathfrak{F}_{t,h}\right],

by the same calculation as earlier.
Thus, by Freedman’s inequality and a union bound, we have that, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\sum_{t<k}Y_{t}(h,f)-\sum_{t<k}\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}\left[\left(\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat}}f_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}f_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{2}\right]\right\rvert

(96)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\iota\sum_{t<k}\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}\left[\left(\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat}}f_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}}_{\phi}f_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{2}\right]}+\iota\right),

(97)

where
\iota=\log(\abs*{\cF}HK/\delta)
. Recalling the misspecification assumption
Lemma

H.4
, this implies that

\sum_{t<k}Y_{t}(h,f)\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}+\iota\right),

for all
h,f,k
, with high probability. Applying this to
f=f^{\scriptscriptstyle{(k)}}
concludes the result.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

H.6
.
We use similar arguments to the preceding lemma. Let
Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h}\coloneqq Q^{\star}_{M_{\texttt{lat}},h}
. The aim is to show that, for all
h\in[H],k\in[K],g\in\mathcal{G}
, we have:

\sum_{t<k}\left(g(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h}-Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}-\left(Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h}-Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}\geq-\beta,

from which the conclusion will follow. We show that

\addcontentsline{lla}{section}{\numberline q:latent-golf-optimism-1}\sum_{t<k}\underbrace{\left(g(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h}-Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h+1}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}-\left(\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{\phi}Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h}-Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}}_{\coloneqq W_{t}(h,g)}\geq-\beta/2,

(98)

and also that

\addcontentsline{lla}{section}{\numberline q:latent-golf-optimism-2}\sum_{t<k}\underbrace{\left(\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{\phi}Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h}-Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}-\left(Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h},a^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h})-r^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h}-Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h}(s^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}_{h+1})\right)^{2}}_{\coloneqq V_{t}(h)}\geq-\beta/2.

(99)

For
Eq. (
98
)
, note that

\addcontentsline{lla}{section}{\numberline q:non-neg-w-t}\En\left[W_{t}(h,g)\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,h}\right]=\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{\phi}\left[\left(g_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{\phi,h}Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h}(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{2}\right],

(100)

and that
\texttt{Var}\left[W_{t}(h,g)\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,h}\right]\leq 16\En\left[W_{t}(h,g)\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,h}\right]
. By Freedman, this gives

\left\lvert\sum_{t<k}W_{t}(h,g)-\sum_{t<k}\En\left[W_{t}(h,g)\mid\mathfrak{F}_{t,h}\right]\right\rvert\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\iota\sum_{t<k}\En\left[W_{t}(h,g)\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,h}\right]}+\iota\right)\leq\frac{1}{2}\En\left[W_{t}(h,g)\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,h}\right]+\mathcal{O}(\iota),

or in other words

\sum_{t<k}W_{t}(h,g)\geq\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t<k}\En\left[W_{t}(h,g)\mid\mathfrak{F}_{t,h}\right]-\mathcal{O}(\iota)\geq-\mathcal{O}(\iota),

using the non-negativity of
Eq. (
100
)
.
For
Eq. (
99
)
, note that

\addcontentsline{lla}{section}{\numberline q:non-neg-v-t}\En\left[V_{t}(h)\mid\mathfrak{F}_{t,h}\right]=-\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{\phi}\left[\left(\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat},h}Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{\phi,h}Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h}\right)^{2}\right]\geq-\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}},

(101)

and that
\texttt{Var}\left[V_{t}(h)\mid\mathfrak{F}_{t,h}\right]\leq 16\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{\phi}\left[\left(\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat},h}Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h}-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{\phi,h}Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h}\right)^{2}\right]
.
By Freedman, this gives

\displaystyle\left\lvert\sum_{t<k}V_{t}(h)-\sum_{t<k}\En\left[V_{t}(h)\mid\mathfrak{F}_{t,h}\right]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\iota\sum_{t<k}\widetilde{\En}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{\phi}\left[\left(\mathcal{T}_{\texttt{lat}}Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h+1}(s_{h},a_{h})-\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}^{\pi^{\scriptscriptstyle{(t)}}}_{\phi,h}Q^{\star}_{\texttt{lat},h+1}(s_{h},a_{h})\right)^{2}\right]}+\iota\right)

(102)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}+\iota\right),

(103)

or in other words

\sum_{t<k}V_{t}(h)\geq\sum_{t<k}\En\left[V_{t}(h)\mid\mathfrak{F}_{t,h}\right]-\mathcal{O}\left(\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}+\iota\right)\geq-\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon_{\mathsf{rep}}+\iota),

where we have used
Eq. (
101
)
.

∎

∎
